table.add_column(style=\"bold cyan\", no_wrap=True)\n    table.add_column()\n    table.add_row(\"protocol\", protocol_version)\n    table.add_row(\"server\", server)\n    table.add_row(\"tools\", str(tools))\n    table.add_row(\"resources\", str(resources))\n    table.add_row(\"prompts\", str(prompts))\n    console.print(Panel(table, title=\"MCP session\", border_style=\"cyan\"))\n\n\ndef print_protocol_frame(direction: str, message: dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    if direction in {\"send\", \"out\", \"client->server\"}:\n        arrow, style = \"-->\", \"bold cyan\"\n    else:\n        arrow, style = \"<--\", \"bold green\"\n\n    kind = \"notification\" if \"id\" not in message else f\"id={message.get('id')}\"\n    method = message.get(\"method\") or (\"result\" if \"result\" in message else \"error\")\n    console.print(Text(f\"{arrow} {method} ({kind})\", style=style))\n    console.print(JSON(json.dumps(message, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)))\n\n\ndef print_protocol_note(text: str) -> None:\n    console.print(Text(text, style=\"dim italic\"))\n\n\ndef print_error(message: str) -> None:\n    console.print(Panel(Text(message), title=\"Error\", border_style=\"red\"))\n"
}

  "content": "\"\"\"Optional OpenTelemetry SDK/exporter setup.\n\nThe MCP SDK already emits spans through the OpenTelemetry API. Installing the\n``otel`` extra and passing ``--otel console`` (or ``--otel otlp``) makes both the\nMCP spans and this project's agent spans visible.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sys\n\nfrom .logging import get_logger\n\nlog = get_logger(\"otel\")\n\n_PROVIDER = None\n\n\ndef configure_otel(mode: str, *, service_name: str = \"react-mcp\") -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Configure a global tracer provider. Returns True when tracing is active.\"\"\"\n    global _PROVIDER\n\n    if mode == \"none\":\n        return False\n    if _PROVIDER is not None:\n        return True\n\n    try:\n        from opentelemetry.sdk.resources import Resource\n        from opentelemetry.sdk.trace import TracerProvider\n        from opentelemetry.sdk.trace.export import BatchSpanProcessor, SimpleSpanProcessor\n        from opentelemetry import trace\n    except ImportError as exc:  # pragma: no cover - depends on optional extra\n        raise RuntimeError(\n            \"OpenTelemetry extra is not installed. Run `uv sync --all-extras` \"\n            \"or `uv add 'react-mcp[otel]'`.\"\n        ) from exc\n\n    provider = TracerProvider(resource=Resource.create({\"service.name\": service_name}))\n\n    if mode == \"console\":\n        from opentelemetry.sdk.trace.export import ConsoleSpanExporter\n\n        provider.add_span_processor(SimpleSpanProcessor(ConsoleSpanExporter(out=sys.stderr)))\n    elif mode == \"otlp\":\n        from opentelemetry.exporter.otlp.proto.grpc.trace_exporter import OTLPSpanExporter\n\n        from ..config import Settings\n\n        endpoint = Settings().otlp_endpoint\n        provider.add_span_processor(BatchSpanProcessor(OTLPSpanExporter(endpoint=endpoint)))\n    else:  # pragma: no cover - guarded by settings literal\n        raise ValueError(f\"Unknown OTel mode: {mode!r}\")\n\n    trace.set_tracer_provider(provider)\n    _PROVIDER = provider\n    log.info(\"OpenTelemetry enabled (mode=%s, service=%s)\", mode, service_name)\n    return True\n\n\ndef shutdown_otel() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Flush and stop the provider, if one was configured.\"\"\"\n    global _PROVIDER\n    if _PROVIDER is not None:\n        try:\n            _PROVIDER.shutdown()\n        except Exception:  # pragma: no cover - best effort\n            log.debug(\"OpenTelemetry shutdown failed\", exc_info=True)\n        _PROVIDER = None\n"
}

def instrumented(fn):
    @functools.wraps(fn)
    def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
        started=time.perf_counter()
        log.info("tool.start name=%s args=%s", fn.__name__, _summarize(kwargs))
        try:
            result=fn(*args, **kwargs)
        except Exception as exc:
            log.warning("tool.error name=%s duration_ms=... error=%s",...)
            raise
        log.info("tool.end name=%s duration_ms=... result_chars=%s", ...)
        return result
    return wrapper
```

def create_server(settings: Settings|None=None) -> MCPServer:
    settings = settings or Settings()
    settings.ensure_dirs()
    server = MCPServer("ReAct Tools", version=__version__, instructions=..., log_level=settings.log_level)
    toolbox = Toolbox(settings)
    register_tools(...)

    return server
```

try:

Every file path is relative\nto that workspace; absolute paths and path traversal are rejected.\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef create_server(settings: Settings | None = None) -> MCPServer:\n    \"\"\"Build a fully registered MCP server (also used by in-memory tests).\"\"\"\n    settings = settings or Settings()\n    settings.ensure_dirs()\n\n    server = MCPServer(\n        \"ReAct Tools\",\n        version=__version__,\n        instructions=INSTRUCTIONS,\n        log_level=settings.log_level,\n    )\n    toolbox = Toolbox(settings)\n    register_tools(server, toolbox)\n    register_resources(server, toolbox, settings)\n    register_prompts(server)\n    return server\n\n\n# Module-level instance so `mcp dev src/react_mcp/server.py` and `mcp run` work.\nmcp = create_server()\n\n\ndef run_server(\n    *,\n    transport: str = \"stdio\",\n    host: str = \"127.0.0.1\",\n    port: int = 8000,\n    settings: Settings | None = None,\n) -> None:\n    settings = settings or Settings()\n    setup_logging(settings.log_level)\n    if settings.otel != \"none\":\n        configure_otel(settings.otel, service_name=\"react-mcp-server\")\n\n    server = create_server(settings)\n    try:\n        if transport == \"stdio\":\n            server.run()\n        elif transport in {\"http\", \"streamable-http\"}:\n            server.run(transport=\"streamable-http\", host=host, port=port)\n        else:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Unknown transport: {transport!r}\")\n    finally:\n        shutdown_otel()\n\n\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> None:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Run the ReAct MCP server.\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--http\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Use Streamable HTTP instead of stdio.\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--host\", default=\"127.0.0.1\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--port\", type=int, default=8000)\n    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\n    run_server(\n        transport=\"http\" if args.http else \"stdio\",\n        host=args.host,\n        port=args.port,\n    )\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    try:\n        main()\n    except KeyboardInterrupt:\n        sys.exit(130)\n"
}

..observability.tracer import Tracer\nfrom .base import MCPClientError, ToolResult, ToolSpec, content_blocks_to_text\n\nlog = get_logger(\"mcp.raw\")\n\n\nclass MCPRPCError(MCPClientError):\n    \"\"\"A JSON-RPC error response.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, code: int, message: str, data: Any = None) -> None:\n        self.code = code\n        self.message = message\n        self.data = data\n        suffix = f\" data={data!r}\" if data is not None else \"\"\n        super().__init__(f\"JSON-RPC error {code}: {message}{suffix}\")\n\n\ndef encode_frame(message: dict[str, Any]) -> bytes:\n    \"\"\"Serialize one JSON-RPC message as a newline-terminated stdio frame.\"\"\"\n    return (json.dumps(message, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(\",\", \":\")) + \"\\n\").encode(\"utf-8\")\n\n\ndef decode_frame(line: bytes | str) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Parse one newline-delimited JSON-RPC frame.\"\"\"\n    if isinstance(line, bytes):\n        line = line.decode(\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")\n    message = json.loads(line)\n    if not isinstance(message, dict):\n        raise MCPClientError(f\"Expected a JSON object, got {type(message).__name__}\")\n    return message\n\n\nclass RawMCPClient:\n    \"\"\"Hand-rolled MCP stdio client with full frame logging.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        settings: Settings | None = None,\n        *,\n        tracer: Tracer | None = None,\n        echo: bool = True,\n        command: list[str] | None = None,\n        timeout: float | None = None,\n    ) -> None:\n        self.settings = settings or Settings()\n        self.tracer = tracer\n        self.echo = echo\n        self.command = command or [sys.executable, \"-m\", \"react_mcp.server\"]\n        self.timeout = timeout or self.settings.request_timeout\n\n        self._proc: asyncio.subprocess.Process | None = None\n        self._reader_task: asyncio.Task[None] | None = None\n        self._stderr_task: asyncio.Task[None] | None = None\n        self._pending: dict[int, asyncio.Future[dict[str, Any]]] = {}\n        self._notifications: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n        self._next_id = 1\n\n        self.protocol_version: str | None = None\n        self.server_info: dict[str, Any] = {}\n        self.capabilities: dict[str, Any] = {}\n        self.instructions: str | None = None\n\n    # -- lifecycle --------------------------------------------------------\n    async def __aenter__(self) -> RawMCPClient:\n        await self.start()\n        return self\n\n    async def __aexit__(self, exc_type: Any, exc: Any, tb: Any) -> None:\n        await self.close()\n\n    async def start(self) -> None:\n        env = {**os.environ, **self.settings.server_env()}\n        log.debug(\"starting server: %s\", \" \".join(self.command))\n        self._proc = await asyncio.create_subprocess_exec(\n            *self.command,\n            stdin=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,\n            stdout=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,\n            stderr=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,\n            env=env,\n            cwd=str(PROJECT_ROOT),\n            limit=4 * 1024 * 1024,\n        )\n        self._reader_task = asyncio.create_task(self._read_loop(), name=\"raw-mcp-reader\")\n        self._stderr_task = asyncio.create_task(self._stderr_loop(), name=\"raw-mcp-stderr\")\n\n    async def close(self) -> None:\n        proc = self._proc\n        if proc is None:\n            return\n        if proc.stdin is not None and not proc.stdin.is_closing():\n            proc.stdin.close()\n ..observability.render import print_protocol_frame\n\n            print_protocol_frame(direction, message)\n\n    async def _send(self, message: dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        if self._proc is None Regex details:
- `_FINAL_RE = re.compile(r"final\s*answer\s*:\s*", re.I)`
- `_ACTION_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*action\s*:\s*([A-Za-z0-9_][A-Za-z0-9_.\-/]*)\s*$", re.I | re.M)` — tool names usually snake_case. Might be `Action: calculator ` with trailing text? Keep anchored. Fallback `_ACTION_INLINE_RE = re.compile(r"\baction\s*:\s*([A-Za-z0-9_][\w.\-/]*)", re.I)` but this could match "Action Input:"; apply only if no anchored match and capture not "Input".
- `_INPUT_RE = re.compile(r"action\s*input\s*:\s*", re.I)`.
- `_THOUGHT_RE = re.compile(r"thought\s*:\s*", re.I)`.

Algorithm:
```python
def parse_react_output(text):
    raw = text or ""
    text2 = _strip_outer_fence(raw.strip())
    final_match = _FINAL_RE.search(text2)
    action_match = _ACTION_RE.search(text2)
    action_input_match = _INPUT_RE.search(text2)

    if final_match and (not action_match or final_match.start() < action_match.start()):
        thought = _slice_thought(text2, final_match.start())
        answer = text2[final_match.end():].strip()
        answer = _clean_final(answer)
        if answer:
            return final
    if action_match:
        name = action_match.group(1).strip()
        thought = _slice_thought(text2, action_match.start())
        raw_input = ""
        if action_input_match and action_input_match.start() >= action_match.end():

    thought = ...
    return invalid

def _prepare_arguments(self, spec, parsed):
    if parsed.arguments is not None:
        if isinstance(parsed.arguments, dict): return parsed.arguments, None
        if isinstance(parsed.arguments, str) and len(spec.properties)==1: ...
        if len(spec.properties)==1:
            return {next(iter(spec.properties)): parsed.arguments}, None
        return None, f"Action Input must be a JSON object for tool ..."
    raw=(parsed.raw_input or "").strip()
    if not raw: return {}, None
    if len(spec.properties)==1:
        prop=next(iter(spec.properties))
        # If raw starts with { or [ but parse failed, report error
        if raw[:1] in "{[":
            return None, parsed.error or "invalid JSON"
        return {prop: raw}, None
    return None, parsed.error or "Action Input is not valid JSON. Expected a JSON object."

  "newString": "def content_blocks_to_text(blocks: list[Any]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Join MCP content blocks into the text a model should read.\n\n    Accepts both SDK objects (``TextContent``) and raw JSON dictionaries, so the\n    same helper works for the official client and the hand-rolled one.\n    \"\"\"\n    parts: list[str] = []\n    for block in blocks or []:\n        if isinstance(block, dict):\n            text = block.get(\"text\")\n            if isinstance(text, str):\n                parts.append(text)\n                continue\n            if \"data\" in block:\n                mime = block.get(\"mimeType\") or block.get(\"mime_type\") or \"binary\"\n                parts.append(f\"[{mime} content, {len(str(block['data']))} chars]\")\n                continue\n            parts.append(json.dumps(block, ensure_ascii=False, default=str))\n            continue\n\n        text = getattr(block, \"text\", None)\n        if isinstance(text, str):\n            parts.append(text)\n            continue\n        data = getattr(block, \"data\", None)\n        if data is not None:\n            mime = getattr(block, \"mime_type\", None) or getattr(block, \"mimeType\", \"binary\")\n            parts.append(f\"[{mime} content, {len(str(data))} chars]\")\n            continue\n        parts.append(str(block))\n    return \"\\n\".join(parts).strip()"

    args = build_parser().parse_args(argv)
    settings = _settings_from_args(args)
    setup_logging(settings.log_level, quiet=args.quiet)
    try:
        if args.command == "serve":
            _cmd_serve(args, settings)
            return 0
        if settings.otel != "none":
            configure_otel(settings.otel, service_name="react-mcp")
        return asyncio.run(_dispatch(args, settings))
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        render.console.print("\n[dim]interrupted[/dim]")
        return 130

        render.print_error(str(exc))
        return 1
    finally:
        shutdown_otel()

notes = await client.read_resource_raw(\"notes://index\")\n            note_text = content_blocks_to_text(list(notes.get(\"contents\", [])))\n            render.print_text(\"notes://index\", note_text[:1500] or \"(empty)\")\n\n            render.print_protocol_note(\"5. prompts/list + prompts/get\")\n            prompts_result = await client.list_prompts_raw()\n            names = [item.get(\"name\") for item in prompts_result.get(\"prompts\", [])]\n            render.print_text(\"prompts\", \"\\n\".join(str(name) for name in names) or \"(none)\")\n            prompt = await client.get_prompt_raw(\"explain_mcp\")\n            render.print_text(\"explain_mcp prompt\", _prompt_text(prompt) or \"(empty)\")\n\n            render.print_protocol_note(\n                \"Raw frames were written to protocol.jsonl in the trace directory.\"\n            )\n        return 0\n    finally:\n        _close_tracer(tracer, {}, quiet=args.quiet)\n\n\nasync def _cmd_doctor(args: argparse.Namespace, settings: Settings) -> int:\n    all_ok = True\n    render.print_doctor_row(\"python\", True, platform.python_version())\n    render.print_doctor_row(\"mcp sdk\", True, _package_version(\"mcp\"))\n    render.print_doctor_row(\"ollama pkg\", True, _package_version(\"ollama\"))\n\n    llm = OllamaLLM(settings)\n    try:\n        models = await llm.list_models()\n        render.print_doctor_row(\n            \"ollama\", True, f\"{len(models)} model(s) at {settings.ollama_host}\"\n        )\n        model_ok = settings.model in models or any(\n            candidate.split(\":\")[0] == settings.model.split(\":\")[0] for candidate in models\n        )\n        detail = settings.model + (\" is installed\" if model_ok else \" is NOT in `ollama list`\")\n        render.print_doctor_row(\"model\", model_ok, detail)\n        all_ok = all_ok and model_ok\n    except LLMError as exc:\n        render.print_doctor_row(\"ollama\", False, str(exc))\n        all_ok = False\n\n    try:\n        protocol = \"?\"\n        async with SDKMCPClient(settings) as client:\n            tools = await client.list_tools()\n            protocol = client.protocol_version or \"?\"\n        render.print_doctor_row(\n            \"mcp server\", True, f\"{len(tools)} tool(s), protocol {protocol}, {settings.transport}\"\n        )\n    except Exception as exc:\n        render.print_doctor_row(\"mcp server\", False, f\"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}\")\n        all_ok = False\n\n    return 0 if all_ok else 1\n\n\nasync def _dispatch(args: argparse.Namespace, settings: Settings) -> int:\n    if args.command == \"tools\":\n        return await _cmd_tools(args, settings)\n    if args.command == \"call\":\n        return await _cmd_call(args, settings)\n    if args.command == \"ask\":\n        return await _cmd_ask(args, settings)\n    if args.command == \"repl\":\n        return await _cmd_repl(args, settings)\n    if args.command == \"protocol-demo\":\n        return await _cmd_protocol_demo(args, settings)\n    if args.command == \"doctor\":\n        return await _cmd_doctor(args, settings)\n    raise ValueError(f\"Unknown command: {args.command!r}\")\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# entrypoint\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n    parser = build_parser()\n    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\n    settings = _settings_from_args(args)\n    setup_logging(settings.log_level, quiet=args.quiet)\n\n    try:\n        if args.command == \"serve\":\n            transport = \"http\" if args.http else settings.transport\n            run_server(\n                transport=transport,\n                host=args.host,\n                port=args.port,\n                settings=settings,\n            )\n            return 0\n\n        if settings.otel != \"none\":\n            configure_otel(settings.otel, service_name=\"react-mcp\")\n        return asyncio.run(_dispatch(args, settings))\n    except KeyboardInterrupt:\n        render.console.print(\"\\n[dim]interrupted[/dim]\")\n        return 130\n    except (LLMError, MCPClientError, RuntimeError, ValueError) as exc:\n        render.print_error(str(exc))\n        return 1\n    except Exception as exc:  # pragma: no cover - unexpected CLI failure\n        render.print_error(f\"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}\")\n        if settings.log_level == \"DEBUG\":\n            raise\n        return 1\n    finally:\n        shutdown_otel()\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())\n"
}

## Objective
- Build a runnable Python learning project: **ReAct agent → local Ollama model → MCP client → MCP server tools**, with an observability layer.
- User approved the plan: default model `qwen3.5:9b`, offline tools + sandboxed files, local JSONL/Rich tracer + optional OpenTelemetry, Python 3.12 via `uv`.

## Important Details

- **Project root:** `/Users/ajayz/Documents/AZ_Code/05_AI_ML/AI_Agents/ReActAgentWithMCP`
- **Environment:** macOS arm64; `uv 0.11.3`; Python 3.12.10 via uv; `.python-version` = `3.12`; `.git` repo created by `uv init` (do **not** commit unless asked).
- **Ollama:** v0.40.13 at `http://localhost:11434`; installed models: `qwen3.5:9b` (default), `llama3.2:latest`, `llama3.2:3b`, `qwen2.5-coder:7b`.
- **Dependencies installed via `uv sync --all-extras`:** `mcp[cli]==2.3.0`, `ollama==0.6.3`, `pydantic-settings`, `rich==15.0.0`; dev: `pytest`, `pytest-asyncio`, `ruff`; optional extra `otel`: `opentelemetry-sdk`, `opentelemetry-exporter-otlp`.
- **MCP v2 facts verified:**
  - High-level server is `mcp.server.MCPServer` (not FastMCP).
  - Client is `mcp.Client(target, *, read_timeout_seconds=..., mode="auto")`; supports `StdioServerParameters` and URL strings.
  - `StdioServerParameters(command, args, env, cwd, ...)` gives child an allow-listed env.
  - `Client.list_tools()`, `call_tool(name, arguments)`, `read_resource(uri)`, `get_prompt(name, args)`.
  - `CallToolResult`: `.content` (TextContent), `.structured_content`, `.is_error`.
  - `Tool`: `.name`, `.title`, `.description`, `.input_schema`.
  - `MCPServer.run()` = stdio; `MCPServer.run(transport="streamable-http", host=..., port=...)`; no `run_async`.
  - `ToolError` from `mcp.server.mcpserver.exceptions`; `ToolAnnotations` has `populate_by_name`.
  - No `mcp.server.middleware` module in v2.
  - Protocol v2 defaults to `server/discover` (2026-07-28); legacy `initialize` (`2025-11-25`) is still served over stdio — raw client intentionally uses legacy handshake for teaching.
- **Verified decorator pattern:** `@mcp.tool()` over `@functools.wraps`-based `@instrumented` preserves signature and generated JSON schema.
- **Ollama API verified:** `AsyncClient.chat(model, messages, stream=False, think=..., options={...}, keep_alive=...)`; response fields include `prompt_eval_count`, `eval_count`, `total_duration`, `message.content`, `message.thinking`.
- **Design decisions:** text-based ReAct (not native tool calling) so trace is visible; stop sequence `["Observation:"]`; default `think=False`; file tools sandboxed under `data/workspace`; no network tools; no arbitrary code execution.

81: def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> None:
82:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Run the ReAct MCP server.")
83:     parser.add_argument("--http", action="store_true", help="Use Streamable HTTP instead of stdio.")
84:     parser.add_argument("--host", default="127.0.0.1")
85:     parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=8000)
86:     args = parser.parse_args(argv)
87:     run_server(
88:         transport="http" if args.http else "stdio",
89:         host=args.host,
90:         port=args.port,
91:     )
92: 
93: 
94: if __name__ == "__main__":
95:     try:
96:         main()
97:     except KeyboardInterrupt:
98:         sys.exit(130)

1: """The ReAct loop: reason, call an MCP tool, observe, repeat."""
2: 
3: from __future__ import annotations
4: 
5: import time
6: from collections.abc import Iterator
7: from contextlib import contextmanager, nullcontext
8: from dataclasses import dataclass, field
9: from typing import Any
10: 
11: from ..config import Settings
12: from ..mcp_client.base import MCPToolClient, ToolSpec
13: from ..observability.logging import get_logger
14: from ..observability.metrics import RunMetrics
15: from ..observability.tracer import Span, Tracer
16: from .llm import OllamaLLM
17: from .parser import ParseResult, parse_react_output
18: from .prompts import FORCE_FINAL_PROMPT, PARSE_RETRY_TEMPLATE, build_system_prompt
19: 
20: log = get_logger("agent")
21: 
22: 
23: @dataclass
24: class AgentResult:
25:     answer: str
26:     steps: int
27:     metrics: RunMetrics
28:     transcript: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
29:     stopped_reason: str = "final_answer"
30:     model: str = ""
31: 
32: 
33: class ReActAgent:
34:     """Text-based ReAct agent whose tools come from an MCP server."""
35: 
36:     def __init__(
37:         self,
38:         *,
39:         llm: OllamaLLM,
40:         client: MCPToolClient,
41:         tools: list[ToolSpec],
42:         settings: Settings,
43:         tracer: Tracer | None = None,
44:         show_steps: bool = True,
45:     ) -> None:
46:         self.llm = llm
47:         self.client = client
48:         self.tools = tools
49:         self.tool_map = {tool.name: tool for tool in tools}
50:         self.settings = settings
51:         self.tracer = tracer
52:         self.show_steps = show_steps
53: 
54:     @contextmanager
55:     def _span(self, name: str, **attributes: Any) -> Iterator[Span | None]:
56:         if self.tracer is None:
57:             with nullcontext(None) as span:
58:                 yield span
59:         else:
60:             with self.tracer.span(name, **attributes) as span:
61:                 yield span
62: 
63:     async def run(self, question: str) -> AgentResult:
64:         metrics = RunMetrics()
65:         transcript: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
66:         messages: list[dict[str, str]] = [
67:             {"role": "system", "content": build_system_prompt(self.tools)},
68:             {"role": "user", "content": question},
69:         ]
70: 
71:         answer = ""
72:         stopped_reason = "max_steps"
73:         parse_retries = 0
74:         last_text = ""
75: 
76:         if self.show_steps:
77:             from ..observability.render import print_question
78: 
79:             print_question(question)
80: 
81:         with self._span(
82:             "agent.run",
83:             question=question,
84:             model=self.settings.model,
85:             max_steps=self.settings.max_steps,
86:         ) as run_span:
87:             for step in range(1, self.settings.max_steps + 1):
88:                 metrics.steps = step
89:                 with self._span("agent.step", step=step) as step_span:
90:                     response = await self.llm.chat(messages, stop=["Observation:"])
91:                     metrics.record_llm(
92:                         prompt_tokens=response.prompt_tokens,
93:                         completion_tokens=response.completion_tokens,
94:                         duration_ms=response.duration_ms,
95:                     )
96:                     text = response.content.strip()
97:                     last_text = text
98:                     parsed = parse_react_output(text)
99:                     transcript.append(
100:                         {
101:                             "step": step,
102:                             "kind": parsed.kind,
103:                             "thought": parsed.thought,
104:                             "raw_output": text,
105:                         }
106:                     )
107:                     if step_span is not None:
108:                         step_span.attributes["parse_kind"] = parsed.kind
109: 
110:                     if parsed.kind == "final":
111:                         answer = parsed.final_answer or ""
112:                         stopped_reason = "final_answer"
113:                         if self.show_steps:
114:                             from ..observability.render import print_step
115: 
116:                             print_step(step=step, thought=parsed.thought, raw_output=None)
117:                         break
118: 
119:                     if parsed.kind == "invalid":
120:                         metrics.parse_retries += 1
121:                         if self.show_steps:
122:                             from ..observability.render import print_step
123: 
124:                             print_step(step=step, thought=parsed.thought, raw_output=text)
125:                         if parse_retries >= self.settings.max_parse_retries:
126:                             stopped_reason = "parse_error"
127:                             break
128:                         parse_retries += 1
129:                         messages.append({"role": "assistant", "content": text or "(empty)"})
130:                         messages.append(
131:                             {
132:                                 "role": "user",
133:                                 "content": PARSE_RETRY_TEMPLATE.format(
134:                                     error=parsed.error or "unknown parse error"
135:                                 ),
136:                             }
137:                         )
138:                         continue
139: 
140:                     execution = await self._execute_action(parsed, metrics)
141:                     transcript[-1].update(
142:                         {
143:                             "action": execution["action"],
144:                             "arguments": execution["arguments"],
145:                             "observation": execution["observation"],
146:                             "is_error": execution["is_error"],
147:                         }
148:                     )
149:                     if self.show_steps:
150:                         from ..observability.render import print_observation, print_step
151: 
152:                         print_step(
153:                             step=step,
154:                             thought=parsed.thought,
155:                             action=execution["action"],
156:                             arguments=execution["arguments"],
157:                         )
158:                         print_observation(
159:                             execution["observation"], is_error=execution["is_error"]
160:                         )
161: 
162:                     messages.append({"role": "assistant", "content": text})
163:                     messages.append(
164:                         {
165:                             "role": "user",
166:                             "content": f"Observation: {execution['observation']}",
167:                         }
168:                     )
169: 
170:                     if step_span is not None:
171:                         step_span.attributes["tool"] = execution["action"]
172: 
173:             if not answer:
174:                 answer, stopped_reason = await self._force_final(
175:                     messages, metrics, stopped_reason, last_text
176:                 )
177: 
178:             metrics.finish()
179:             if run_span is not None:
180:                 run_span.attributes.update(
181:                     {
182:                         "steps": metrics.steps,
183:                         "stop_reason": stopped_reason,
184:                         "answer_chars": len(answer),
185:                         **metrics.as_dict(),
186:                     }
187:                 )
188:             if self.tracer is not None:
189:                 self.tracer.event(
190:                     "agent.finish",
191:                     stop_reason=stopped_reason,
192:                     answer_chars=len(answer),
193:                     **metrics.as_dict(),
194:                 )
195: 
196:         if self.show_steps:
197:             from ..observability.render import print_final
198: 
199:             print_final(answer, steps=metrics.steps)
200: 
201:         return AgentResult(
202:             answer=answer,
203:             steps=metrics.steps,
204:             metrics=metrics,
205:             transcript=transcript,
206:             stopped_reason=stopped_reason,
207:             model=self.settings.model,
208:         )
209: 
210:     async def _execute_action(
211:         self, parsed: ParseResult, metrics: RunMetrics
212:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
213:         action = parsed.action or ""
214:         spec = self.tool_map.get(action)
215:         if spec is None:
216:             metrics.tool_errors += 1
217:             names = ", ".join(sorted(self.tool_map)) or "none"
218:             observation = (
219:                 f"Error: unknown tool {action!r}. Available tools: {names}. "
220:                 "Choose one of those names."
221:             )
222:             if self.tracer is not None:
223:                 self.tracer.event("agent.unknown_tool", tool=action)
224:             return {
225:                 "action": action,
226:                 "arguments": parsed.arguments,
227:                 "observation": observation,
228:                 "is_error": True,
229:             }
230: 
231:         arguments, argument_error = self._prepare_arguments(spec, parsed)
232:         if argument_error is not None:
233:             metrics.tool_errors += 1
234:             observation = f"Error: {argument_error}"
235:             if self.tracer is not None:
236:                 self.tracer.event("agent.bad_arguments", tool=action, error=argument_error)
237:             return {
238:                 "action": action,
239:                 "arguments": parsed.raw_input or parsed.arguments,
240:                 "observation": observation,
241:                 "is_error": True,
242:             }
243: 
244:         started = time.perf_counter()
245:         try:
246:             result = await self.client.call_tool(action, arguments or {})
247:         except Exception as exc:
248:             duration_ms = (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000
249:             metrics.record_tool(duration_ms=duration_ms, is_error=True)
250:             observation = f"Error: tool {action!r} failed: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
251:             log.warning("tool call failed tool=%s error=%s", action, exc)
252:             return {
253:                 "action": action,
254:                 "arguments": arguments,
255:                 "observation": observation,
256:                 "is_error": True,
257:             }
258: 
259:         duration_ms = (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000
260:         metrics.record_tool(duration_ms=duration_ms, is_error=result.is_error)
261:         observation = result.as_observation(limit=self.settings.max_observation_chars)
262:         if result.is_error:
263:             observation = f"Error from tool {action!r}: {observation}"
264:         return {
265:             "action": action,
266:             "arguments": arguments,
267:             "observation": observation,
268:             "is_error": result.is_error,
269:         }
270: 
271:     @staticmethod
272:     def _prepare_arguments(
273:         spec: ToolSpec, parsed: ParseResult
274:     ) -> tuple[dict[str, Any] | None, str | None]:
275:         value = parsed.arguments
276:         properties = spec.properties
277: 
278:         if value is not None:
279:             if isinstance(value, dict):
280:                 return value, None
281:             if len(properties) == 1:
282:                 return {next(iter(properties)): value}, None
283:             return None, (
284:                 f"Action Input for tool {spec.name!r} must be a JSON object, "
285:                 f"not {type(value).__name__}."
286:             )
287: 
288:         raw = (parsed.raw_input or "").strip()
289:         if not raw:
290:             return {}, None
291:         if len(properties) == 1:
292:             if raw[:1] in "{[":
293:                 return None, parsed.error or "Action Input is not valid JSON."
294:             return {next(iter(properties)): raw}, None
295:         return None, (
296:             f"Action Input for tool {spec.name!r} must be a JSON object with keys: "
297:             f"{', '.join(properties) or '(none)'}."
298:         )
299: 
300:     async def _force_final(
301:         self,
302:         messages: list[dict[str, str]],
303:         metrics: RunMetrics,
304:         stopped_reason: str,
305:         last_text: str,
306:     ) -> tuple[str, str]:
307:         log.debug("forcing a final answer after %s", stopped_reason)
308:         messages.append({"role": "user", "content": FORCE_FINAL_PROMPT})
309:         with self._span("agent.force_final", reason=stopped_reason):
310:             response = await self.llm.chat(messages, stop=["Observation:"])
311:             metrics.record_llm(
312:                 prompt_tokens=response.prompt_tokens,
313:                 completion_tokens=response.completion_tokens,
314:                 duration_ms=response.duration_ms,
315:             )
316:         parsed = parse_react_output(response.content)
317:         if parsed.kind == "final" and parsed.final_answer:
318:             return parsed.final_answer, "forced_final"
319:         if response.content.strip():
320:             return response.content.strip(), "unparsed_final"
321:         return last_text or "I could not produce an answer within the step limit.", "step_limit"

<content>
1: """MCP client built on the official Python SDK."""
2: 
3: from __future__ import annotations
4: 
5: import sys
6: from pathlib import Path
7: from typing import Any
8: 
9: from mcp import Client, StdioServerParameters
10: 
11: from ..config import PROJECT_ROOT, Settings
12: from ..observability.logging import get_logger
13: from ..observability.tracer import Tracer
14: from .base import ToolResult, ToolSpec, content_blocks_to_text
15: 
16: log = get_logger("mcp.client")
17: 
18: 
19: class SDKMCPClient:
20:     """Thin, instrumented wrapper around ``mcp.Client``.
21: 
22:     Supports both transports:
23:     - ``stdio``: launches ``python -m react_mcp.server`` as a subprocess.
24:     - ``http``: connects to a Streamable HTTP endpoint (``react-mcp serve --http``).
25:     """
26: 
27:     def __init__(
28:         self,
29:         settings: Settings,
30:         *,
31:         tracer: Tracer | None = None,
32:         transport: str | None = None,
33:         url: str | None = None,
34:     ) -> None:
35:         self.settings = settings
36:         self.tracer = tracer
37:         self.transport = transport or settings.transport
38:         self.url = url or settings.server_url
39:         self._client: Client | None = None
40:         self._cm: Any = None
41:         self.protocol_version: str | None = None
42:         self.server_name: str | None = None
43: 
44:     def _target(self) -> Any:
45:         if self.transport == "http":
46:             return self.url
47:         env = self.settings.server_env()
48:         return StdioServerParameters(
49:             command=sys.executable,
50:             args=["-m", "react_mcp.server"],
51:             env=env,
52:             cwd=str(PROJECT_ROOT),
53:         )
54: 
55:     async def __aenter__(self) -> SDKMCPClient:
56:         target = self._target()
57:         if self.tracer is not None:
58:             with self.tracer.span(
59:                 "mcp.connect",
60:                 kind="client",
61:                 transport=self.transport,
62:                 target=self.url if self.transport == "http" else "stdio://react_mcp.server",
63:             ):
64:                 await self._connect(target)
65:         else:
66:             await self._connect(target)
67:         return self
68: 
69:     async def _connect(self, target: Any) -> None:
70:         self._cm = Client(
71:             target,
72:             read_timeout_seconds=self.settings.request_timeout,
73:             mode="auto",
74:         )
75:         self._client = await self._cm.__aenter__()
76:         self.protocol_version = self._client.protocol_version
77:         info = self._client.server_info
78:         self.server_name = info.name if info is not None else "unknown"
79:         log.info(
80:             "connected server=%s protocol=%s transport=%s",
81:             self.server_name,
82:             self.protocol_version,
83:             self.transport,
84:         )
85: 
86:     async def __aexit__(self, exc_type: Any, exc: Any, tb: Any) -> None:
87:         if self.tracer is not None:
88:             with self.tracer.span("mcp.disconnect", kind="client"):
89:                 await self._disconnect()
90:         else:
91:             await self._disconnect()
92: 
93:     async def _disconnect(self) -> None:
94:         if self._cm is not None:
95:             await self._cm.__aexit__(None, None, None)
96:         self._cm = None
97:         self._client = None
98: 
99:     def _require_client(self) -> Client:
100:         if self._client is None:
101:             raise RuntimeError("MCP client is not connected; use 'async with SDKMCPClient(...)'.")
102:         return self._client
103: 
104:     async def list_tools(self) -> list[ToolSpec]:
105:         client = self._require_client()
106:         if self.tracer is not None:
107:             with self.tracer.span("mcp.tools/list", kind="client") as span:
108:                 result = await client.list_tools()
109:                 span.attributes["tool_count"] = len(result.tools)
110:         else:
111:             result = await client.list_tools()
112: 
113:         specs = [
114:             ToolSpec(
115:                 name=tool.name,
116:                 title=tool.title,
117:                 description=tool.description or "",
118:                 input_schema=tool.input_schema or {},
119:             )
120:             for tool in result.tools
121:         ]
122:         log.debug("tools listed count=%d", len(specs))
123:         return specs
124: 
125:     async def call_tool(self, name: str, arguments: dict[str, Any]) -> ToolResult:
126:         client = self._require_client()
127:         attributes = {
128:             "mcp.tool.name": name,
129:             "arguments": arguments,
130:         }
131:         if self.tracer is not None:
132:             with self.tracer.span("mcp.tools/call", kind="client", **attributes) as span:
133:                 result = await client.call_tool(name, arguments)
134:                 span.attributes["is_error"] = bool(result.is_error)
135:                 span.attributes["content_blocks"] = len(result.content or [])
136:                 self.tracer.event(
137:                     "mcp.tool.result",
138:                     tool=name,
139:                     is_error=bool(result.is_error),
140:                 )
141:         else:
142:             result = await client.call_tool(name, arguments)
143: 
144:         text = content_blocks_to_text(list(result.content or []))
145:         return ToolResult(
146:             content=text,
147:             is_error=bool(result.is_error),
148:             structured=result.structured_content,
149:         )
150: 
151:     async def read_resource(self, uri: str) -> str:
152:         client = self._require_client()
153:         if self.tracer is not None:
154:             with self.tracer.span("mcp.resources/read", kind="client", uri=uri):
155:                 result = await client.read_resource(uri)
156:         else:
157:             result = await client.read_resource(uri)
158:         return content_blocks_to_text(list(result.contents or []))
159: 
160:     async def list_resources(self) -> list[str]:
161:         client = self._require_client()
162:         if self.tracer is not None:
163:             with self.tracer.span("mcp.resources/list", kind="client"):
164:                 result = await client.list_resources()
165:         else:
166:             result = await client.list_resources()
167:         return [str(resource.uri) for resource in result.resources]
168: 
169:     async def list_prompts(self) -> list[str]:
170:         client = self._require_client()
171:         if self.tracer is not None:
172:             with self.tracer.span("mcp.prompts/list", kind="client"):
173:                 result = await client.list_prompts()
174:         else:
175:             result = await client.list_prompts()
176:         return [prompt.name for prompt in result.prompts]
177: 
178:     async def get_prompt(self, name: str, arguments: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> str:
179:         client = self._require_client()
180:         if self.tracer is not None:
181:             with self.tracer.span("mcp.prompts/get", kind="client", prompt=name):
182:                 result = await client.get_prompt(name, arguments)
183:         else:
184:             result = await client.get_prompt(name, arguments)
185:         return "\n".join(
186:             content_blocks_to_text([message.content]) for message in result.messages
187:         ).strip()
188: 
189: 
190: def default_server_command() -> list[str]:
191:     """Convenience for docs/tests: the stdio command the client launches."""
192:     return [sys.executable, "-m", "react_mcp.server"]
193: 
194: 
195: def project_relative(path: Path) -> str:
196:     try:
197:         return str(path.relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT))
198:     except ValueError:
199:         return str(path)

48: def _summarize(value: Any, limit: int = 120) -> str:

53: def instrumented(fn):
54:     """Log tool entry/exit on the server's stderr without changing its signature."""
55: 
56:     @functools.wraps(fn)
57:     def wrapper(*args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
58:         started = time.perf_counter()
59:         log.info("tool.start name=%s args=%s", fn.__name__, _summarize(kwargs))
60:         try:
61:             result = fn(*args, **kwargs)
62:         except Exception as exc:
63:             duration = (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000
64:             log.warning(
65:                 "tool.error name=%s duration_ms=%.1f error=%s: %s",
66:                 fn.__name__,
67:                 duration,
68:                 type(exc).__name__,
69:                 exc,
70:             )
71:             raise
72:         duration = (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000
73:         log.info("tool.end name=%s duration_ms=%.1f", fn.__name__, duration)
74:         return result
75: 
76:     return wrapper

79: class Toolbox:
80:     """Stateful implementation behind the MCP tools."""
81: 
82:     def __init__(self, settings: Settings) -> None:
83:         self.settings = settings
84:         self.settings.ensure_dirs()
85: 
86:     # -- calculator -------------------------------------------------------
87:     def calculator(self, expression: str) -> str:
88:         expression = expression.strip()
89:         if not expression:
90:             raise ToolError("The expression is empty.")
91:         if len(expression) > MAX_EXPRESSION_CHARS:
92:             raise ToolError(f"Expression is too long (max {MAX_EXPRESSION_CHARS} characters).")
93:         try:
94:             tree = ast.parse(expression, mode="eval")
95:         except SyntaxError as exc:
96:             raise ToolError(f"Could not parse expression: {exc.msg}") from exc
97: 
98:         value = self._eval_node(tree.body)
99:         if isinstance(value, float):
100:             if value != value or value in (float("inf"), float("-inf")):
101:                 raise ToolError("Result is not a finite number.")
102:             if abs(value) > MAX_ABS_RESULT:
103:                 raise ToolError("Result is too large.")
104:             return format(value, ".12g")
105:         return str(value)
106: 
107:     def _eval_node(self, node: ast.AST) -> int | float:
108:         if isinstance(node, ast.Constant):
109:             if isinstance(node.value, bool) or not isinstance(node.value, (int, float)):
110:                 raise ToolError("Only integer and float constants are allowed.")
111:             return node.value
112:         if isinstance(node, ast.BinOp) and type(node.op) in _BIN_OPS:
113:             left = self._eval_node(node.left)
114:             right = self._eval_node(node.right)
115:             if isinstance(node.op, ast.Pow):
116:                 if abs(right) > MAX_EXPONENT:
117:                     raise ToolError(f"Exponent is too large (max {MAX_EXPONENT}).")
118:                 if abs(left) > 1e6:
119:                     raise ToolError("Base is too large for exponentiation.")
120:             try:
121:                 result = _BIN_OPS[type(node.op)](left, right)
122:             except ZeroDivisionError as exc:
123:                 raise ToolError("Division by zero.") from exc
124:             if isinstance(result, (int, float)) and abs(result) > MAX_ABS_RESULT:
125:                 raise ToolError("Intermediate result is too large.")
126:             return result
127:         if isinstance(node, ast.UnaryOp) and type(node.op) in _UNARY_OPS:
128:             return _UNARY_OPS[type(node.op)](self._eval_node(node.operand))
129:         raise ToolError(
130:             f"Unsupported expression element: {type(node).__name__}. "
131:             "Only numbers and + - * / // % ** parentheses are allowed."
132:         )
133: 
134:     # -- time -------------------------------------------------------------
135:     def current_time(self, timezone: str) -> str:
136:         try:
137:             zone = ZoneInfo(timezone)
138:         except (ZoneInfoNotFoundError, ValueError) as exc:
139:             raise ToolError(f"Unknown timezone {timezone!r}. Try 'UTC' or 'America/New_York'.") from exc
140:         now = datetime.now(zone)
141:         return now.isoformat(timespec="seconds") + f" ({now.strftime('%A')})"
142: 
143:     # -- notes ------------------------------------------------------------
144:     def note_files(self) -> list[Path]:
145:         if not self.settings.notes_dir.exists():
146:             return []
147:         return sorted(path for path in self.settings.notes_dir.glob("*.md") if path.is_file())
148: 
149:     def note_title(self, path: Path) -> str:
150:         try:
151:             for line in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
152:                 if line.startswith("# "):
153:                     return line[2:].strip()
154:         except OSError:
155:             pass
156:         return path.stem.replace("_", " ").replace("-", " ").title()
157: 
158:     def note_index(self) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
159:         return [{"name": path.stem, "title": self.note_title(path)} for path in self.note_files()]
160: 
161:     def _note_path(self, name: str) -> Path:
162:         stem = name[:-3] if name.lower().endswith(".md") else name
163:         if not _NAME_RE.match(stem) or ".." in stem:
164:             raise ToolError(f"Invalid note name {name!r}.")
165:         path = (self.settings.notes_dir / f"{stem}.md").resolve()
166:         root = self.settings.notes_dir.resolve()
167:         if root != path and root not in path.parents:
168:             raise ToolError("Note path escapes the notes directory.")
169:         if not path.is_file():
170:             available = ", ".join(item["name"] for item in self.note_index()) or "none"
171:             raise ToolError(f"No note named {stem!r}. Available notes: {available}.")
172:         return path
173: 
174:     def read_note(self, name: str) -> str:
175:         return self._note_path(name).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
176: 
177:     def search_notes(self, query: str, limit: int) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
178:         terms = re.findall(r"[a-z0-9]+", query.lower())
179:         if not terms:
180:             raise ToolError("Search query must contain at least one word.")
181:         results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
182:         for path in self.note_files():
183:             try:
184:                 body = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
185:             except OSError:
186:                 continue
187:             title = self.note_title(path)
188:             haystack = f"{title}\n{body}".lower()
189:             title_lower = title.lower()
190:             score = sum(
191:                 (3 * title_lower.count(term)) + haystack.count(term) for term in set(terms)
192:             )
193:             if score == 0:
194:                 continue
195:             lowered = body.lower()
196:             position = min(
197:                 (lowered.find(term) for term in terms if lowered.find(term) >= 0),
198:                 default=0,
199:             )
200:             start = max(position - 60, 0)
201:             snippet = body[start : start + 200].replace("\n", " ").strip()
202:             results.append(
203:                 {
204:                     "name": path.stem,
205:                     "title": title,
206:                     "score": score,
207:                     "snippet": snippet + ("..." if start + 200 < len(body) else ""),
208:                 }
209:             )
210:         results.sort(key=lambda item: (-item["score"], item["name"]))
211:         return results[:limit]
212: 
213:     # -- filesystem sandbox ----------------------------------------------
214:     def _safe_path(self, relative: str, *, must_exist: bool = False) -> Path:
215:         if "\x00" in relative:
216:             raise ToolError("Path contains a null byte.")
217:         candidate = Path(relative)
218:         if candidate.is_absolute():
219:             raise ToolError("Absolute paths are not allowed; use paths relative to the workspace.")
220:         root = self.settings.workspace_dir.resolve()
221:         resolved = (root / candidate).resolve()
222:         if resolved != root and root not in resolved.parents:
223:             raise ToolError("Path escapes the workspace directory.")
224:         if must_exist and not resolved.exists():
225:             raise ToolError(f"Path does not exist: {relative!r}")
226:         return resolved
227: 
228:     def list_files(self, relative: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
229:         target = self._safe_path(relative or ".", must_exist=True)
230:         root = self.settings.workspace_dir.resolve()
231:         if target.is_file():
232:             return [
233:                 {
234:                     "path": target.relative_to(root).as_posix(),
235:                     "type": "file",
236:                     "size": target.stat().st_size,
237:                 }
238:             ]
239:         entries: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
240:         for path in sorted(target.rglob("*")):
241:             if len(entries) >= 200:
242:                 entries.append({"path": "...", "type": "truncated", "size": 0})
243:                 break
244:             if path.is_file():
245:                 entries.append(
246:                     {
247:                         "path": path.relative_to(root).as_posix(),
248:                         "type": "file",
249:                         "size": path.stat().st_size,
250:                     }
251:                 )
252:             elif path.is_dir():
253:                 entries.append(
254:                     {"path": path.relative_to(root).as_posix(), "type": "dir", "size": 0}
255:                 )
256:         return entries
257: 
258:     def read_file(self, relative: str, max_bytes: int) -> str:
259:         path = self._safe_path(relative, must_exist=True)
260:         if not path.is_file():
261:             raise ToolError(f"Not a file: {relative!r}")
262:         raw = path.read_bytes()
263:         truncated = len(raw) > max_bytes
264:         text = raw[:max_bytes].decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
265:         if truncated:
266:             text += f"\n\n[truncated at {max_bytes} bytes; file is {len(raw)} bytes]"
267:         return text
268: 
269:     def write_file(self, relative: str, content: str) -> str:
270:         if len(content) > MAX_WRITE_CHARS:
271:             raise ToolError(f"Content is too large (max {MAX_WRITE_CHARS} characters).")
272:         path = self._safe_path(relative)
273:         if path == self.settings.workspace_dir.resolve():
274:             raise ToolError("Refusing to overwrite the workspace directory.")
275:         path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
276:         path.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")
277:         return f"Wrote {len(content)} characters to {path.relative_to(self.settings.workspace_dir.resolve()).as_posix()}."
278:

{

  "content": "# MCP protocol in one page\n\nThe Model Context Protocol (MCP) is a JSON-RPC 2.0 protocol between a **host**\n(the application), a **client** (one connection to one server), and a **server**\n(the process that exposes capabilities).\n\n## Transports\n\n- **stdio** - the client launches the server as a subprocess and exchanges\n  newline-delimited JSON on stdin/stdout. stderr is free for logs. This project\n  uses stdio by default, which is why the client launches\n  `python -m react_mcp.server`.\n- **Streamable HTTP** - the server listens on an HTTP endpoint and the client\n  POSTs JSON-RPC messages. Run `react-mcp serve --http` to try it.\n\n## Lifecycle\n\n1. **Initialize** - the client sends `initialize` with its protocol version,\n   capabilities, and client info. The server replies with its own protocol\n   version, capabilities, and server info. Both sides must agree on a version.\n2. **Initialized notification** - the client sends `notifications/initialized`\n   to say the handshake is complete.\n3. **Discovery** - `tools/list`, `resources/list`, and `prompts/list` enumerate\n   what the server offers.\n4. **Use** - `tools/call` invokes a tool; `resources/read` fetches a resource;\n   `prompts/get` renders a prompt template.\n5. **Shutdown** - the client closes the transport and the server exits.\n\nNewer protocol revisions also support a `server/discover` request, which lets a\nclient learn the server's capabilities without the older initialize handshake.\nThis project's official-SDK client negotiates automatically; the raw client in\n`mcp_client/raw_client.py` deliberately performs the legacy `initialize` version\n(protocol revision `2025-11-25`) so the frames are easy to study.\n\n## A tools/call request\n\n```json\n{\"jsonrpc\":\"2.0\",\"id\":2,\"method\":\"tools/call\",\n \"params\":{\"name\":\"calculator\",\"arguments\":{\"expression\":\"(17 * 3) + 9\"}}}\n```\n\nThe result contains a `content` array of typed blocks. This server returns a\nsingle text block, plus `structuredContent` when the tool returns JSON data.\nAn `isError` flag marks tool-level failures; protocol-level failures use a\nJSON-RPC `error` object instead.\n\n## Capabilities are opt-in\n\nA server only advertises what it implements. This server advertises tools,\nresources, prompts, and logging. A client should check the capability flags\nbefore calling the corresponding methods.\n\n## Security notes\n\n- Tool arguments are untrusted input. Validate them on the server, and never\n  `eval` them.\n- Bind HTTP servers to `127.0.0.1` unless you add authentication and TLS.\n- The client controls which server command it launches; do not run an MCP\n  server from an untrusted source.\n"
}
```

{
  "filePath": "/Users/ajayz/Documents/AZ_Code/05_AI_ML/AI_Agents/ReActAgentWithMCP/data/notes/react.md",
  "content": "# The ReAct pattern\n\nReAct means **Reason + Act**. Instead of asking a model for an answer in one\nshot, the loop alternates between a short reasoning trace and an action whose\nresult is fed back into the context.\n\nThe classic text format is:\n\n```text\nThought: I need to evaluate the arithmetic expression.\nAction: calculator\nAction Input: {\"expression\": \"(17 * 3) + 9\"}\n```\n\nThe runtime executes the tool and appends:\n\n```text\nObservation: 60\n```\n\nThe model then either takes another action or finishes with:\n\n```text\nThought: I now know the final answer.\nFinal Answer: 60\n```\n\n## Why text instead of native tool calling?\n\nNative tool calling hides the decision inside an opaque API field. A text\nprotocol makes every Thought, Action, and Observation visible in the transcript\nand in the trace files, which is the point of this learning project. The trade\noff is that the model can emit malformed JSON, so the parser must be forgiving\nand the agent needs a retry path.\n\n## Loop mechanics in this project\n\n1. The system prompt lists every MCP tool, its description, and its JSON schema.\n2. The model is asked to stop at `Observation:` so it cannot invent tool output.\n3. `parse_react_output` extracts a Thought plus either an Action/Action Input or\n   a Final Answer. Fenced code, surrounding prose, and single-string inputs are\n   tolerated.\n4. The agent calls `tools/call` over MCP, formats the observation, and appends\n   both the assistant message and a user message containing the observation.\n5. The loop ends on a Final Answer, the step limit, an unrecoverable parse\n   error, or a forced-final pass that asks the model to answer with what it has.\n\n## Failure modes\n\n- **Unknown tool** - the observation lists the valid names and the model retries.\n- **Bad arguments** - missing keys or invalid JSON produce a corrective message.\n- **Tool error** - reported as `Error from tool ...`, so the model can recover.\n- **Runaway loop** - `REACT_MCP_MAX_STEPS` bounds the work.\n- **Wrong answer** - the deterministic tools still return the truth in the\n  observation; the model's job is to use it, not to recompute it mentally.\n"
}
```

{
  "filePath": "/Users/ajayz/Documents/AZ_Code/05_AI_ML/AI_Agents/ReActAgentWithMCP/data/notes/ollama.md",
  "content": "# Running models locally with Ollama\n\nOllama serves open-weight models over a small HTTP API. The Python package\n`ollama` wraps that API with an `AsyncClient`.\n\n## Useful commands\n\n```bash\nollama list\nollama pull qwen3.5:9b\nollama run qwen3.5:9b\nollama ps\n```\n\nThe API lives at `http://localhost:11434` by default. Point the project at a\ndifferent host with `REACT_MCP_OLLAMA_HOST`.\n\n## Chat requests\n\nThis project sends a `messages` list with `system`, `user`, and `assistant`\nroles. Two options matter most:\n\n- `temperature` - lower values make tool arguments more repeatable. The project\n  defaults to `0.2`.\n- `num_ctx` - the context window. Tool schemas, the transcript, and observations\n  all consume it, so long sessions eventually need a larger value.\n\nOther knobs include `num_predict` (maximum generated tokens), `top_p`, and\n`keep_alive` (how long the model stays loaded). The client sets `keep_alive` so\nrepeated questions in the REPL do not reload the model each time.\n\nSome newer models can emit a separate `thinking` channel. It is disabled by\ndefault here because it adds latency and tokens; enable it with `--think`.\n\n## Tokens and timing\n\nThe response reports `prompt_eval_count`, `eval_count`, and duration fields in\nnanoseconds. The agent records prompt/completion tokens and per-call latency in\nits run metrics so you can see where time goes.\n\n## Troubleshooting\n\n- `Connection refused` - Ollama is not running; start `ollama serve`.\n- `model not found` - run `ollama pull <model>` or pick a model from\n  `ollama list`.\n- Very slow first call - the model is loading into memory. Later calls reuse it\n  while `keep_alive` holds it.\n- Context overflow - raise `REACT_MCP_NUM_CTX` or lower the step limit.\n- Nonsense tool syntax - try a larger model or a lower temperature; the parser\n  retries once or twice before giving up.\n"
}
```

{
  "filePath": "/Users/ajayz/Documents/AZ_Code/05_AI_ML/AI_Agents/ReActAgentWithMCP/data/notes/observability.md",
  "content": "# Observability: traces, spans, and metrics\n\nAn agent is a distributed system in miniature: the LLM call, the MCP client,\nthe MCP server subprocess, and the tools all participate in one user request.\nObservability means being able to reconstruct that request after the fact.\n\n## What this project records\n\nEach CLI run gets a directory under `traces/<timestamp>-<run-name>/`:\n\n- `trace.jsonl` - one JSON object per span start/end plus standalone events.\n- `protocol.jsonl` - raw MCP JSON-RPC frames, when protocol echo is enabled.\n- `summary.json` - run metadata, final metrics, and any error.\n\nSpans are nested. A typical `ask` run looks like:\n\n```text\nagent.run\n  agent.step\n    llm.chat\n    mcp.tools/call\n  agent.finish\n```\n\nEach span carries attributes such as the model, tool name, arguments, token\ncounts, or parse result. Durations are measured with a monotonic clock.\n\n## Metrics\n\nThe agent counts steps, LLM calls, prompt/completion tokens, tool calls, tool\nerrors, parse retries, and wall-clock time. Token counts come from Ollama; they\nare estimates of model work, not billing units, because the model runs locally.\n\n## Optional OpenTelemetry\n\nThe tracer can also emit spans to an OTel exporter. Install the extra with\n`uv sync --all-extras`, then run with `--otel console` or `--otel otlp`. The\nlocal JSONL trace is always the source of truth; OTel is for connecting this\ntoy to a real backend such as Jaeger, Tempo, or Honeycomb.\n\n## Inspecting a run\n\n```bash\nls traces\njq -c 'select(.event==\"span.end\") | {name:.span.name,ms:.span.duration_ms}' traces/*/trace.jsonl\n```\n\nBecause traces are plain JSONL, they are easy to diff, grep, and feed into a\nnotebook. Nothing is uploaded anywhere.\n"
}
```

  "content": "# Welcome to the ReAct + MCP workspace\n\nThis project wires four pieces together:\n\n1. **Ollama** runs a local model such as `qwen3.5:9b`.\n2. **A ReAct agent** prompts the model to alternate between Thought and Action.\n3. **An MCP client** discovers and calls tools on a server.\n4. **An MCP server** implements those tools, resources, and prompts locally.\n\n## What the tools can do\n\n- `calculator` - safe arithmetic, no `eval`.\n- `get_current_time` - current time in an IANA timezone.\n- `search_notes` / `read_note` - search and read the Markdown notes in\n  `data/notes`.\n- `list_files` / `read_file` / `write_file` - work inside `data/workspace`, with\n  path traversal rejected.\n\nResources expose the note index (`notes://index`), individual notes\n(`notes://<name>`), and the redacted agent config (`config://agent`). Prompts\ninclude `research` and `explain_mcp`.\n\n## Try these questions\n\n- \"What is (17 * 3) + 9, and what time is it in UTC?\"\n- \"Search the notes for observability and summarize what a span is.\"\n- \"Read the react note and explain why this agent uses text instead of native\n  tool calling.\"\n- \"List the workspace files, then create `hello.txt` with a short greeting.\"\n\n## Where things live\n\n- `src/react_mcp/server.py` - MCP server and tool registration.\n- `src/react_mcp/agent/` - prompts, parser, Ollama wrapper, ReAct loop.\n- `src/react_mcp/mcp_client/` - official-SDK client and a raw JSON-RPC client.\n- `src/react_mcp/observability/` - tracing, metrics, and Rich rendering.\n- `traces/` - one folder per run.\n- `data/workspace/` - the only place file tools may write.\n"
}

<type>file</type>
<content>
1: """A minimal MCP client written directly against JSON-RPC 2.0.
2: 
3: This deliberately does not use the SDK. It exists to make the wire visible:
4: 
5: - newline-delimited JSON over the server's stdin/stdout (the stdio transport),
6: - the ``initialize`` handshake used by MCP versions through 2025-11-25,
7: - ``notifications/initialized``, ``tools/list`` and ``tools/call``.
8: 
9: The SDK's default client negotiates the newer ``server/discover`` flow; the
10: server serves both eras. The legacy handshake is easier to read and is what most
11: MCP material still documents, so it is the one this client implements.
12: """
13: 
14: from __future__ import annotations
15: 
16: import asyncio
17: import json
18: import os
19: import sys
20: from pathlib import Path
21: from typing import Any
22: 
23: from ..config import PROJECT_ROOT, Settings
24: from ..observability.logging import get_logger
25: from ..observability.tracer import Tracer
26: from .base import MCPClientError, ToolResult, ToolSpec, content_blocks_to_text
27: 
28: log = get_logger("mcp.raw")
29: 
30: 
31: class MCPRPCError(MCPClientError):
32:     """A JSON-RPC error response."""
33: 
34:     def __init__(self, code: int, message: str, data: Any = None) -> None:
35:         self.code = code
36:         self.message = message
37:         self.data = data
38:         suffix = f" data={data!r}" if data is not None else ""
39:         super().__init__(f"JSON-RPC error {code}: {message}{suffix}")
40: 
41: 
42: def encode_frame(message: dict[str, Any]) -> bytes:
43:     """Serialize one JSON-RPC message as a newline-terminated stdio frame."""
44:     return (json.dumps(message, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n").encode("utf-8")
45: 
46: 
47: def decode_frame(line: bytes | str) -> dict[str, Any]:
48:     """Parse one newline-delimited JSON-RPC frame."""
49:     if isinstance(line, bytes):
50:         line = line.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
51:     message = json.loads(line)
52:     if not isinstance(message, dict):
53:         raise MCPClientError(f"Expected a JSON object, got {type(message).__name__}")
54:     return message
55: 
56: 
57: class RawMCPClient:
58:     """Hand-rolled MCP stdio client with full frame logging."""
59: 
60:     def __init__(
61:         self,
62:         settings: Settings | None = None,
63:         *,
64:         tracer: Tracer | None = None,
65:         echo: bool = True,
66:         command: list[str] | None = None,
67:         timeout: float | None = None,
68:     ) -> None:
69:         self.settings = settings or Settings()
70:         self.tracer = tracer
71:         self.echo = echo
72:         self.command = command or [sys.executable, "-m", "react_mcp.server"]
73:         self.timeout = timeout or self.settings.request_timeout
74: 
75:         self._proc: asyncio.subprocess.Process | None = None
76:         self._reader_task: asyncio.Task[None] | None = None
77:         self._stderr_task: asyncio.Task[None] | None = None
78:         self._pending: dict[int, asyncio.Future[dict[str, Any]]] = {}
79:         self._notifications: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
80:         self._next_id = 1
81: 
82:         self.protocol_version: str | None = None
83:         self.server_info: dict[str, Any] = {}
84:         self.capabilities: dict[str, Any] = {}
85:         self.instructions: str | None = None
86: 
87:     # -- lifecycle --------------------------------------------------------
88:     async def __aenter__(self) -> RawMCPClient:
89:         await self.start()
90:         return self
91: 
92:     async def __aexit__(self, exc_type: Any, exc: Any, tb: Any) -> None:
93:         await self.close()
94: 
95:     async def start(self) -> None:
96:         env = {**os.environ, **self.settings.server_env()}
97:         log.debug("starting server: %s", " ".join(self.command))
98:         self._proc = await asyncio.create_subprocess_exec(
99:             *self.command,
100:             stdin=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
101:             stdout=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
102:             stderr=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
103:             env=env,
104:             cwd=str(PROJECT_ROOT),
105:             limit=4 * 1024 * 1024,
106:         )
107:         self._reader_task = asyncio.create_task(self._read_loop(), name="raw-mcp-reader")
108:         self._stderr_task = asyncio.create_task(self._stderr_loop(), name="raw-mcp-stderr")
109: 
110:     async def close(self) -> None:
111:         proc = self._proc
112:         if proc is None:
113:             return
114:         if proc.stdin is not None and not proc.stdin.is_closing():
115:             proc.stdin.close()
116:         try:
117:             await asyncio.wait_for(proc.wait(), timeout=5)
118:         except TimeoutError:
119:             proc.kill()
120:             await proc.wait()
121: 
122:         for task in (self._reader_task, self._stderr_task):
123:             if task is not None:
124:                 task.cancel()
125:         self._reader_task = None
126:         self._stderr_task = None
127:         self._proc = None
128: 
129:         for future in self._pending.values():
130:             if not future.done():
131:                 future.set_exception(MCPClientError("MCP server process closed"))
132:         self._pending.clear()
133: 
134:     # -- io ---------------------------------------------------------------
135:     async def _read_loop(self) -> None:
136:         assert self._proc is not None and self._proc.stdout is not None
137:         stdout = self._proc.stdout
138:         while True:
139:             line = await stdout.readline()
140:             if not line:
141:                 break
142:             stripped = line.strip()
143:             if not stripped:
144:                 continue
145:             try:
146:                 message = decode_frame(stripped)
147:             except (json.JSONDecodeError, MCPClientError) as exc:
148:                 log.warning("unparseable server frame: %s", exc)
149:                 self._record("recv", {"raw": stripped.decode("utf-8", "replace")})
150:                 continue
151:             self._record("recv", message)
152: 
153:             message_id = message.get("id")
154:             if message_id is not None and message_id in self._pending:
155:                 future = self._pending[message_id]
156:                 if not future.done():
157:                     future.set_result(message)
158:             elif "method" in message:
159:                 self._notifications.append(message)
160:                 log.debug("server notification: %s", message.get("method"))
161:             else:
162:                 log.debug("unmatched server frame: %s", message)
163: 
164:         for future in self._pending.values():
165:             if not future.done():
166:                 future.set_exception(MCPClientError("MCP server closed the connection"))
167:         self._pending.clear()
168: 
169:     async def _stderr_loop(self) -> None:
170:         assert self._proc is not None and self._proc.stderr is not None
171:         while True:
172:             line = await self._proc.stderr.readline()
173:             if not line:
174:                 break
175:             log.debug("server stderr: %s", line.decode("utf-8", "replace").rstrip())
176: 
177:     def _record(self, direction: str, message: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
178:         if self.tracer is not None:
179:             self.tracer.protocol(direction, message, echo=self.echo)
180:         elif self.echo:
181:             from ..observability.render import print_protocol_frame
182: 
183:             print_protocol_frame(direction, message)
184: 
185:     async def _send(self, message: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
186:         if self._proc is None or self._proc.stdin is None:
187:             raise MCPClientError("MCP server is not running")
188:         self._record("send", message)
189:         self._proc.stdin.write(encode_frame(message))
190:         await self._proc.stdin.drain()
191: 
192:     # -- JSON-RPC ---------------------------------------------------------
193:     async def request(
194:         self, method: str, params: dict[str, Any] | None = None
195:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
196:         if self._proc is None:
197:             raise MCPClientError("MCP server is not running")
198:         request_id = self._next_id
199:         self._next_id += 1
200:         message: dict[str, Any] = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": request_id, "method": method}
201:         if params is not None:
202:             message["params"] = params
203: 
204:         future: asyncio.Future[dict[str, Any]] = asyncio.get_running_loop().create_future()
205:         self._pending[request_id] = future
206:         try:
207:             await self._send(message)
208:             response = await asyncio.wait_for(future, timeout=self.timeout)
209:         except TimeoutError as exc:
210:             raise MCPClientError(f"Timed out waiting for {method!r} after {self.timeout}s") from exc
211:         finally:
212:             self._pending.pop(request_id, None)
213: 
214:         if "error" in response:
215:             error = response["error"] or {}
216:             raise MCPRPCError(
217:                 int(error.get("code", -32603)),
218:                 str(error.get("message", "Unknown error")),
219:                 error.get("data"),
220:             )
221:         result = response.get("result")
222:         return result if isinstance(result, dict) else {"result": result}
223: 
224:     async def notify(self, method: str, params: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> None:
225:         message: dict[str, Any] = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "method": method}
226:         if params is not None:
227:             message["params"] = params
228:         await self._send(message)
229: 
230:     # -- MCP verbs --------------------------------------------------------
231:     async def initialize(self, *, client_name: str = "react-mcp-raw-client") -> dict[str, Any]:
232:         result = await self.request(
233:             "initialize",
234:             {
235:                 "protocolVersion": "2025-11-25",
236:                 "capabilities": {},
237:                 "clientInfo": {"name": client_name, "version": "0.1.0"},
238:             },
239:         )
240:         self.protocol_version = result.get("protocolVersion")
241:         self.server_info = result.get("serverInfo") or {}
242:         self.capabilities = result.get("capabilities") or {}
243:         self.instructions = result.get("instructions")
244:         await self.notify("notifications/initialized")
245:         return result
246: 
247:     async def list_tools_raw(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
248:         return await self.request("tools/list", {})
249: 
250:     async def call_tool_raw(self, name: str, arguments: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
251:         return await self.request("tools/call", {"name": name, "arguments": arguments})
252: 
253:     async def list_resources_raw(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
254:         return await self.request("resources/list", {})
255: 
256:     async def read_resource_raw(self, uri: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
257:         return await self.request("resources/read", {"uri": uri})
258: 
259:     async def list_prompts_raw(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
260:         return await self.request("prompts/list", {})
261: 
262:     async def get_prompt_raw(
263:         self, name: str, arguments: dict[str, str] | None = None
264:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
265:         return await self.request("prompts/get", {"name": name, "arguments": arguments or {}})
266: 
267:     # -- normalized interface (same as SDKMCPClient) ----------------------
268:     async def list_tools(self) -> list[ToolSpec]:
269:         result = await self.list_tools_raw()
270:         return [
271:             ToolSpec(
272:                 name=tool.get("name", ""),
273:                 title=tool.get("title"),
274:                 description=tool.get("description", ""),
275:                 input_schema=tool.get("inputSchema", tool.get("input_schema", {})),
276:             )
277:             for tool in result.get("tools", [])
278:         ]
279: 
280:     async def call_tool(self, name: str, arguments: dict[str, Any]) -> ToolResult:
281:         result = await self.call_tool_raw(name, arguments)
282:         content = result.get("content") or []
283:         text = content_blocks_to_text(content)
284:         return ToolResult(
285:             content=text,
286:             is_error=bool(result.get("isError", result.get("is_error", False))),
287:             structured=result.get("structuredContent", result.get("structured_content")),
288:         )
289: 
290:     def drain_notifications(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
291:         notifications = self._notifications
292:         self._notifications = []
293:         return notifications
294: 
295: 
296: def server_path() -> Path:
297:     return PROJECT_ROOT / "src" / "react_mcp" / "server.py"

(End of file - total 297 lines)
</content>
